Paragraph (5) 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER IV 
PAYMENT OF STAMP DUTY PAYABLE 
 
Article 12 
 
(1) 
Payment of Stamp Duty payable on Documents shall be made by using: 
a. 
Stamp; or 
b. 
tax payment slip 
(2) 
The stamp as referred to in paragraph (1) letter a is in the form of: 
a. 
Patch stamp; 
b. 
Electronic stamp; or 
c. 
Stamp in other forms as stipulated by the Minister 
(3) 
Everyone is obliged to obtain a permit to make a Stamp in another form 
as referred to in paragraph (2) letter c 
(4) 
Further provisions on payment of the outstanding Stamp Duty as referred 
to in paragraph (1) shall be regulated in the Ministerial Regulation 
(5) 
Any procurement, management and sales of Stamp as referred to in 
paragraph (2) shall be regulated by or based on the Government 
Regulation 
 
Elucidation of Article 12 
Paragraph (1) 
Letter a 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Letter b 
Payments of Stamp Duty can also be made by using a tax payment slip in the 
event the mechanism for payment of Stamp Duty using a Stamp is deemed 
inefficient or even impossible. For example, for Documents that will be used as 
evidence in the court in a large quantity, the payment for which is through 
Postdated Duty Stamping as regulated in this Law. The granting of alternatives 
in payment of Stamp Duty is intended to provide convenience in the Stamp Duty 
payment 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
337 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
Paragraph (2) 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (3) 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (4) 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (5) 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER V 
PATCH STAMP, ELECTRONIC STAMP, AND STAMPS IN OTHER FORMS 
 
Article 13 
 
(1) 
Patch stamp as referred to in Article 12 paragraph (2) letter a has general 
characteristics and special characteristics 
(2) 
The general characteristics as referred to in paragraph (1) at least 
contain: 
a. 
image of the state emblem Garuda Pancasila; 
b. 
a phrase “Meterai Tempel” (“Patch Stamp”); and 
c. 
numbers indicating the nominal value 
(3) 
Each Patch stamp, in addition to having the general characteristics as 
referred to in paragraph (2), also has special characteristics as a security 
element that is incorporated in the design, materials, and printing 
technique. 
(4) 
The special characteristics of the Patch stamp as referred to in paragraph 
(3) can be overt, semi-covert, and covert/forensic) in nature 
(5) 
Further provisions on the determination of the general characteristics and 
the special characteristics of the Patch stamp as referred to in paragraph 
(2), paragraph (3), and paragraph (4) as well as their enforcement shall 
be regulated in the Ministerial Regulation. 
 
Elucidation of Article 13 
Paragraph (1) 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (2) 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
338 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (3) 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (4) 
“Overt” means a characteristic of the Patch stamp that can be identified without 
using any tools 
“Semicovert” means a characteristic of the Patch stamp that can be identified by 
using a tool 
“Covert/forensic” means a characteristic of the Patch stamp that can be 
identified only through a forensic examination. 
Paragraph (5) 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 14 
 
(1) 
Electronic stamp as referred to in Article 12 paragraph (2) letter b has a 
unique code and specific information 
(2) 
Provisions on the unique code and certain information as referred to in 
paragraph (1) are regulated in the Ministerial Regulation 
 
Elucidation of Article 14 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 15 
 
(1) 
Stamps in other forms stipulated by the Minister as referred to in Article 
12 paragraph (2) letter c are Stamps made by using a digital Stamp 
imprint machine, computerized system, printing technology, and other 
systems or technologies. 
(2) 
Further provisions on Stamps in other forms as referred to in paragraph 
(1) shall be regulated in the Ministerial Regulation 
 
Elucidation of Article 15 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 16 
 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
339 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
(1) 
The Government has the authority to determine the validity of Stamps as 
referred to in Article 12 paragraph (2) 
(2) 
Provisions on the procedure for determining the validity of Stamps as 
referred to in paragraph (1) shall be regulated in the Ministerial 
Regulation 
 
Elucidation of Article 16 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER VI 
POSTDATED DUTY-STAMPING 
 
Article 17 
 
(1) 
Postdated Duty Stamping is carried out for: 
a. 
Documents as referred to in Article 3 paragraph (2) for which the 
Stamp Duty is unpaid or underpaid; and/or  
b. 
Documents that are used as evidence in the court as referred to in 
Article 3 paragraph (1) letter b 
(2) 
Party who is obliged to pay the Stamp Duty through the Postdated Duty 
Stamping as referred to in paragraph (1) is the Indebted Party as referred 
to in Article 9 
 
Elucidation of Article 17 
Paragraph (1) 
Self-explanatory. 
Paragraph (2) 
In principle, party who is obliged to pay the Stamp Duty through a Postdated 
Duty Stamping is the Indebted Party. However, in the implementation, payments 
of Stamp Duty through the Postdated Duty Stamping can  be made by the 
Document holder either as an Indebted Party or non-Indebted Party. 
 
Article 18 
 
(1) 
Stamp Duties that are obliged to be paid through Postdated Duty 
Stamping as referred to in Article 17 are determined at the amount of: 
a. 
Stamp Duty payable on the Documents as referred to in Article 17 
paragraph (1) letter a plus the administrative sanction; and 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
340 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
b. 
Stamp Duty payable on the Documents as referred to in Article 17 
paragraph (1) letter b 
(2) 
The administrative sanction as referred to in paragraph (1) letter a is 
100% (one hundred percent) of the Stamp Duty payable 
 
Elucidation of Article 18 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 19 
 
(1) 
To the Indebted Party who does not pay or underpay the Stamp Duty 
payable is issued with a tax assessment letter in accordance with the 
provisions of the statutory regulations on the general provisions and 
procedures of taxation. 
(2) 
The amount of the underpaid Stamp Duty in the tax assessment letter as 
referred to in paragraph (1) is equal to the Stamp Duty that is unpaid or 
underpaid plus the administrative sanction as referred to in Article 18 
paragraph (2) 
 
Elucidation of Article 19 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 20 
 
Provisions on the procedure for payment of Stamp Duty through the Postdated 
Duty Stamping shall be regulated in the Ministerial Regulation 
 
Elucidation of Article 20 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER VII 
PROHIBITIONS FOR THE AUTHORIZED OFFICIALS 
 
Article 21 
 
(1) 
The authorized officials are, in performing their duty or position, 
prohibited from: 
a. 
receiving, considering, or keeping any of the Documents as referred 
to in Article 3 for which the Stamp Duty is unpaid or underpaid; 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
341 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
b. 
attaching any of the Documents as referred to in Article 3 for which 
the Stamp Duty is unpaid or underpaid to other related Documents; 
c. 
making copies, carbon copies, counterparts, or excerpts of any of 
the Documents as referred to in Article 3 for which the Stamp Duty 
is unpaid or underpaid; and/or  
d. 
giving a remark or note on any of the Documents as referred to in 
Article 3 for which the Stamp Duty is unpaid or underpaid 
(2) 
Violation of the provisions as referred to in paragraph (1) is subject to 
administrative sanctions in accordance with the provisions of the statutory 
regulations 
 
Elucidation of Article 21 
Paragraph (1) 
This article is intended to the effect that each of the authorized officials, in 
performing their duty or position, contributes in ensuring that the Stamp Duty 
payable on the Document has been paid properly. 
The authorized officials in this provision include judges, registrars, bailiffs, 
notaries, Land Deed Officials, state civil apparatus, members of the Indonesian 
National Armed Forces/State Police of the Republic of Indonesia, and state 
officials. 
The statutory regulations in this provision are, for an example, the statutory 
regulations governing the discipline and obligations or prohibition for state civil 
apparatus, state officials, or other public officials. 
Paragraph (2) 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER VIII 
FACILITY OF EXEMPTION FROM THE STAMP-DUTY IMPOSITION 
 
Article 22 
 
(1) 
Stamp Duty payable may be granted an exemption from the Stamp Duty 
imposition, either temporarily or permanently, for: 
a. 
Documents as referred to in Article 3 that state a transfer of rights 
to the land and/or building to the effect of accelerating the process 
of handling and recovering the socio-economic conditions of a 
region resulting due to natural calamities that are designated as 
natural disasters; 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
342 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
b. 
Documents as referred to in Article 3 which state a transfer of right 
to the land and/or buildings that is used to carry out activities that 
are solely religious and/or social in nature that are not commercial 
in nature; 
c. 
Documents as referred to in Article 3 to the effect of encouraging or 
implementing 
the 
government 
programs 
and/or 
policies 
of 
competent institutions in the monetary or the financial service 
sectors; and/or  
d. 
Documents as referred to in Article 3 related to the implementation 
of international agreements that have been binding based on the 
provisions of statutory regulations on the international agreements 
or based on the reciprocal principle 
(2) 
Further provisions on the granting of exemption from the Stamp Duty 
imposition as referred to in paragraph (1) shall be regulated by or based 
on the Government Regulation 
 
Elucidation of Article 22 
Paragraph (1) 
Letter a 
Self-explanatory. 
Letter b 
“Land and/or buildings that is used to carry out activities that are solely religious 
and/or social in nature that are not commercial in nature” means the entire or 
part of the land and/or building that is only used to carry out religious worship 
activities or social activities, such as, orphanages or nursing homes that are not 
commercial in nature or not for profit 
Letter c 
“Policy of the competent institution in the of financial service sector” means, 
among other things, in terms of: 
a. 
carrying out the deepening or development of the financial services 
sector; 
b. 
carrying out restructuring and maintaining the sustainability of financial 
service institutions; and/or  
c. 
encouraging 
the 
intermediation 
function 
of 
the 
financial 
service 
institutions 
Letter d 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
343 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
“International agreement” means an international agreement between Indonesia 
and 1 (one) or more countries, or and international institutions/organizations, 
that are subject to the international laws. 
Paragraph (2) 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 23 
 
Stamp Duty payable expires after a period of 5 (five) years as of the time it is 
payable 
 
Elucidation of Article 23 
Viewed from the legal certainty aspect, the expiration of 5 (five) years which is 
calculated as from the time when the Stamp Duty is payable, applies to all 
Documents 
 
CHAPTER IX 
CRIMINAL PROVISIONS 
 
Article 24 
 
Everyone who: 
a. 
imitates or counterfeits any Stamp issued by the Government of the 
Republic of Indonesia with the intention of using or requesting another 
person to use the Stamp as an original, not counterfeited, or valid Stamp; 
or 
b. 
with the same purpose as referred to in letter a, making a Stamp by using 
the original seal unlawfully, including making an electronic Stamp as 
referred to in Article 14 and Stamp in other forms as referred to in Article 
15, unlawfully, 
shall be punished with imprisonment of a maximum of 7 (seven) years and a 
fine in maximum of Rp. 500.000.000,00 (five hundred million rupiah). 
 
Elucidation of Article 24 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 25 
 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
344 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
Everyone who uses, sells, offers, delivers, has inventory for sale, or imports into 
the territory of the Republic of Indonesia: 
a. 
Any forged or illegally made stamp as if it is genuine, not counterfeited, 
and made in a lawful manner; or 
b. 
goods that are affixed with a Stamp as referred to in letter a, as if they 
are  original, not counterfeited, and made in a lawful manner, 
shall be sentenced with imprisonment up to maximum of 7 (seven) years and a 
fine in maximum of Rp. 500.000.000,00 (five hundred million rupiah). 
 
Elucidation of Article 25 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 26 
 
Everyone who: 
a. 
removes any mark that is used to indicate that a Stamp can no longer be 
used on any Stamp of the Government of the Republic of Indonesia that 
has been used with the intention to use it or to ask another person to use 
it as if such a Stamp had never been used; 
b. 
with the same intension as referred to in letter a, removes the Signature, 
characteristic, or the mark when a Stamp of Government of the Republic 
of Indonesia has been used, provided that the prevailing provision of the 
statutory regulations must be affixed above or on such a Stamp; or 
c. 
uses, sells, offers, delivers, has inventory for sale, or imports into the 
territory of the Republic of Indonesia any Stamp the mark, the Signature, 
the characteristic, or the usage date of which is removed, as if such a 
Stamp had never been used, 
shall be sentenced with imprisonment up to 3 (three) years or a fine in 
maximum of Rp. 200.000.000,00 (two hundred million rupiah). 
 
Elucidation of Article 26 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER X 
MISCELLANEOUS 
 
Article 27 
 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
345 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
As to any matters which have not been specifically regulated in this Law, the 
provisions in the statutory regulations on the general provisions and procedures 
of taxation shall apply. 
 
Elucidation of Article 27 
Self-explanatory. 
 
CHAPTER XI 
TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS  
 
Article 28 
 
Upon the coming into effect of this Law: 
a. 
Any Documents for which the Stamp Duty is unpaid or underpaid, that 
were made prior to this Law comes into effect, their Stamp Duty is still 
payable and shall be paid based on Law Number 13 of 1985 regarding 
Stamp Duty 
b. 
Patch stamps that have been printed based on Law Number 13 of 1985 
regarding Stamp Duty and the implementing regulations thereof, that are 
remaining, can still be used until 1 (one) year as of this Law comes into 
effect and cannot be exchanged for money or in any form 
c. 
Patch stamps that are used to make payments of Stamp Duty payable on 
the Documents as referred to in letter b, can be used with the total value 
of Stamp Duty affixed to a Document of at least Rp. 9.000,00 (nine 
thousand rupiah) 
 
Elucidation of Article 28 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 29 
 
The procedure for payment of the Stamp Duty payable under this Law, which is 
paid using Patch stamp(s) that has been printed based on Law Number 13 of 
1985 regarding Stamp Duty and the implementing regulations thereof as 
referred to in Article 28 letter b, shall be regulated in the Ministerial Regulation. 
 
Elucidation of Article 29 

COMPILATION INTO SINGLE DOCUMENT  
Of Law On General Provisions and Procedures of Taxation 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
346 
 
 
 
www.pajak.go.id 
 
 
“Implementing regulations thereof” mean the implementing regulations of Law 
Number 13 of 1985 regarding Stamp Duty which govern the shape, size, and 
color of the most current Patch stamp. 
 
CHAPTER XII 
CLOSING PROVISION 
 
Article 30 
 
Upon the coming into effect of this Law, all of the statutory regulations that are 
the implementation of Law Number 13 of 1985 regarding Stamp Duty (State 
Gazette of the Republic of Indonesia of 1985 Number 69, Supplement to State 
Gazette of the Republic of Indonesia Number 3313), are declared remain in 
effect as long as not contradictory to the provisions in this Law 
 
Elucidation of Article 30 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 31 
 
Upon the coming into effect of this Law, Law Number 13 of 1985 regarding 
Stamp Duty (State Gazette of the Republic of Indonesia of 1985 Number 69, 
Supplement to State Gazette of the Republic of Indonesia Number 3313), is 
revoked and declared null and void. 
 
Elucidation of Article 31 
Self-explanatory. 
 
Article 32 
 
This law comes into effect as of 1 January 2021 
 
Elucidation of Article 32 
Self-explanatory. 
 
For public cognizance, orders the publication of this Law by placing it in the 
State Gazette of the Republic of Indonesia
